5a1(pa): @#pa'i dkyil 'khor 'di dag thams cad la thugs mnyam pa nyid du cig gi sgo nas gzigs so rnam par gzig so//sman tu gzigaso//de ltang klong stanas/dkyil 'khor 'di dgra thams cad sgru bas ma las 'das par

8b2(pa): skur bkod cing //rigs 'thun dkyil 'khor kun gsal 'gyur//sngags kyi 'od zer sgra bcas pas//chos can bcu drug dag pa'i dbyings ka ring gi gnas su bkod byas nas//mtshan mchog sum cu rtsa gsum rdzogs//de bzhin ka nas

8b4(pa): nyid sku'i gnas bzhugs pas//dpe byad bzang po brgyad cu rdzogs//gzhan yang rin chen sna bdun dang //bkra shis brgyad sogs kun gyis spras//khams le rnam sbrul tshe mtha' yas ke zhal gcig phyag gnyis dkar mar mdangs//pad+ma rtsa
8b5(pa): dadang gral gri 'dzin//chos kyi snang ba'i brgyan gyis grubs//pad+ma brgyan 'bring rang bzhin mchog/shar gyi dkyil 'khor gnas la bzhugs//gang las phrul pa'i chos dbang phyugru/dmar ser zhal gcig phyag bzhi pa//pad+ma'i 'phreng ba bum pa 'dzin
8b6(pa): lho'i dkyil 'khor gnas ba zhugs pa'o//g+haM las 'phrul pa'i chags med 'od//zhal cig phyag bzhi byi ru'i mdog/par gnas gleg bam me long 'dzin//nub kyi dkyil 'khor gnas bzhugs pa'o//dam las 'phrul pa'i spyan dag/
8b7(pa): pa/zhal gcig phyag bzhi btsod kyi mdog/rin chen 'phreng ba bsil yab 'dzin//byang gis dkyil 'khor gnas bzhugs pa'o//tsam las 'phrul pa mi spyod pa//zhal gcig phyag bzhi chu shel mdog/rdo rje dril 'dzin 'khril ba'i
9a1(pa): tshul/dpal be'u bum rtseg 'od phreng brgyan//tsham las 'phrul pa'i mngon snang mdzad//zhal gcig phyag bzhi dkar ti la//rin chen dra ba gleg bam 'dzin//shar gyi dkyil 'khor gnas la bzhugs//dzam la
9a2(pa): 'phrul pa'i brtan dbang phyug/zhal gcig phyag bzhi bai DUR+Yaka/rin chen snye ma dung phor 'dzin//lho'i dkyil gnas la bzhugs//dz+haiM las 'phrul pa'i 'brug sgra ni//zhal gcig phyag bzhi gur gum mdog/rin chen chags shing gaN+DI
9a3(pa): 'dzin//nub kyi dkyil 'khor gnas la bzhugs//nyam las 'phrul pa'i dri mod dpal//zhal gcig phyag bzhi margad mdog/rin chen pad spungs za ma tog/byeng gis dkyil 'khor gnas la bzhugs//TaM las 'phrul pa'i rin chen srungs///
9a4(pa): zhal gcig phyag gnyis gser gyis mdog/rin chen dril bu yum dang 'khril//rin chen mdzod srungs 'khor lor rtsegs//tham las sprul pa'i na mkha'i mdog/zhal gcig phyag bzhi sngo skya'i mdangs//rin chen gling bu pad+mo 'dzin//shar
9a5(pa): gyi dkyil 'khor gnas la bzhugs//raM las 'phrul pa'i dra ba can//zhal gcig phyug bzhi'i mthing ka'i mdog/rin chen 'phreng dang bsrung skud 'dzin//lho'i dkyil 'khor gnas la bzhugs//b+h+rU~M las 'phrul pa'i dkon mchog rgyus//zhal gA mi//
9a6(pa): phyug bani dmar skya'i mdangs//chos kyi rgyal mtshan rgyud mangs 'dzin//nub kyi dkyil 'khor gnas la bzhugs//NaM las sprul ba'i rin chen tog/zhal gcig phyug bzhi ma reg mdog/dgos 'dod bum pa dpag bsas shing //byang gi
9a7(pa): dkyil 'khor gnas la bzhugs//taM las sprul pa kun tu snang //zhal gcig phyag bzhi gser gyi mdog/'khor lo dril 'dzin yum dang 'khril//gtsug tor dri med rdor phyed 'dzin//tha las sprul pa'i 'od kyi dpal//zhal gcig phyag
9b1(pa): bzhi shel gyi mdog/'khor lo du byas ze 'dzin//shar gyi dkyil 'kho gnas la bzungs//da pa sprul pa dag pang cug+sa la gAm+b+hi byag pani nyis mangs//'kha cwang rgyalas can dgu ba dzod //tshul gyil 'a
9b2(pa): la bzhugs//d+hu las sprul pa'i cho 'phrul bkod//zhal gcig phyag bzhi dmar skya'i mdangs//nyi ma sI phyed rdo rje sbrus//nub kyi dkyil 'khor gnas la bzhugs//na las sprul pa'i rnam par gzigs//zhal gcig phyag bzhi lngangs skya'i
9b3(pa): mdangs//ku sha'i chun po a mra'i 'bras//byang gi dkyil 'khor gnas la bzhugasoka pa las sprul pa'i don yod po//zhal gcig phyag bzhi ljang sngon mdog/rgya gram dril bcas yum dang 'khril//rdo gdengs po ral gri 'dzin//
9b4(pa): pha las sprul ba'i brtson 'grus shugs//zhal gcig phyag bzhi ka ra ta ri//rgya gram phreng ba rtse gsum 'dzin /shar gyi dkyil 'khor gnas la bzhugs//ba las sprul pa'i las chen po//zhal gcig phyag bzhi ser skya'i
9b5(pa): mdog/pid+ma 'phreng ba nor bu 'dzin//lha'i dkyil 'khor gnas la bzhugs//b+ha las sprul pa'i gnod sbyin 'dul//zhal gcig phyag bzhi pad+ma'i mdangs//phyag ni rdo rje mche ba 'dzin//nub kyi dkyil 'khor gnas l-i bzhugs/
9b6(pa): ma las sprul pa'i sna tshogs dbang //zhal gcig phyag gzhi ldangs ser mdangs//zla ba rin chen dbyug pa 'dzin//byang gi dkyil 'khor gnas la bzhugs//bod gshegs rgyal ba de dag kyang //'khor los brgyur rgyal cha yugs
9b7(pa): dadang mtshan dang dpe byad sna tshogs brgyan//rig mthun btsun mos mdzes byas pa'o//ya las byams pa'i dbang phyug ni//zhal gcig phyag gzhi gangs ri'i mdog/rdo rje sdengs dbyug ral gri dang //tri shU thogs bcas pad+ma'i dbyug
10a1(pa): s+yA p+na sa ga/drug pa na pa / sa sgyu ba nga /ga d+hag na gas kyi / na drug pa du khyod badz+ra ts+tsha

10a3(pa): gri gug gtun shing 'phreng ba 'dzin//rigs kyis phrengs pas mdzes byas nus//khro bcas rngam pa'i mdangs bcas pas//lho la sogs pa'i sgo bzhi ru ka/gzhan 'joms drag po'i las kyi 'khod//sha las sprul pa'i sbyin dbang phyu/

10a7(pa): bde ba'i blo gros te//zhal gcig phyag bzhi na mkha'i mdangs//yum bcas rdo rje dri bu dang //na mkha'i mdzod sprin 'od dkyil 'khor//kha las rgya mtsho'i sgra dbyangs can//zhal gcig phyag bzhi pu sha ru//rin chen pi wang phreng ba dang //rnga yab
10b1(pa): rdo rje cong pata thogs/ga las gzi brjid bkod pa ni ka zhal gcig phyag bzhi skya skyegs mdog/pad+ma'i lcags sa zla ba dang //'khor lo yid bzhin bum pa thogs//g+ha las 'khor lo rngon pa ni//zha la gcig phyag bzhi mar dang //'khor lo /

10b5(pa): sa kyi 'khor lo sgrog byed pa'o//wa las sgyu ma'i sprul pa ni//zhal gcig phyag bzhi pa la sha/seng+ge rgya mtsho rin chen rdzing //rin chen bum pa bad bcu de bcas//Ta las rin chen rgyal po ni//zhal gcig phyag bzhi ra ga'i mdangs//ri bo
10b6(pa): pad+ma chu gri dang //gser nya phyo ba'i rdzing bu 'dzin//D+ha las sprul pa'i dra ba can//zhal gcig phyag bzhi ljang khu'i mdog/rgya gram dril bu phreng ba dang //b+han d+ha s+yAd yab 'dzag pa'i rgyun//ta las sprul pa'i lag bzangs ni//zhal gcig phyag bzhi kar

11a1(pa): @#gzhal gcig phyag bzhi rA ga'i mdog/pad+ma sngon po dpag bsam shing /kha dang rtse gsum zla byed gzugs/nA las chos kyi mthong ba ni/am+b+ha gcig phyag ba'i sku tshogs su//go ma se she la smon pa dang /

11b1(pa): bad+ha sde ldang dang //rin chen phreng ba 'i ltu kAr/mamyor pa'i lus nga na 'dun su/kA sa sulas sbrul sgru /d+mo ches byed la gags pa /gan sbyis phyag gnyis gas gzugs nga/ smras/

11b3(pa): dbus kyi dkyil 'khor gyi'o//oM tsakra A lo ka na mo//rak+Sha sam b+ha ra rak+Sha saM b+ha ra d+ha na hri ti pa yA tI dza swA hA/nub kyi dkyil 'khor bgyi'o//oM badz+ra d+ha ri pa kr-i ta ya nI shaMr ti pa sha ma ni dza swA hA/shar gyi dkyil 'khor bgyi'o//
11b4(pa): oM rad na mu ku ti ku man ta ban+d+ha du na du na d+hi ba dza hUM taM dza swA hA/lho phyogs kyi dkyil 'khor gyi'o//oM bi shwa tshin d+ha ma ma du ru ha ha ha ha sarba kur d+ha ni hUM phaT dza sUhA/byang gi dkyil 'khor gyi'o//oM ma ma A ba lo ki nu ru ru s+phu ru d+ha/
11b5(pa): nA sid+ha bo d+hi satwa ma ma hUM dza swA hUM//rigs kyi byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi'o//oM bI lo ka nA moHsid+d+hi ma sa ku ru ku ru bo d+hi satwa b+ha ra gu na hU~M dzA swAhA//rigs las sprul pa'i byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi'o//oM d+he/

12a1(pa): gsum bsal byed pa'o//tAM nge 'dzin la snyoms bzhugs na//dkyil 'khod byin brlabs dbang bskur zhing //rigs kyi bdag po gsal bar mdzad//gnas kun ma nan+ta ma yongs dag nag/nas gsum snang

12a3(pa): mAm ni/um+b+hi bi ma la hUM hUM hUM/shwa ra~M shud+d+ha sau na//sngags gsum gyis gsal bar byed pa'o//oM bai sho tshan d+ha ma ni a be sam bo d+hi/ma ma ku ru sa~M la ha sa~M na sin bin mud pad+ma da ra hU~M hUM hUM/rnam par sprul pa'i sngag so//oM maN+Dal/
12a4(pa): ni bi shwa tsakra mu ku Ta rbud d+ha hi na ma pi la ru kram sam hUM phaT swA hA//dkyil 'khor ba'i sngag so//oM a a bin du she ma la shan dun d+he ta thA ga ta bo d+hi pad+ma ku ru AHhUM swA hA//rtsa ba'i sngag so//oM pe ne mu khams pad+ma saM ba ri waM
12a5(pa): ku ru hUM hUM ha~M he d+ha ra sha ra swAhA//snying po'i sngag so//oM ni ma su t+yai b+h+yo ga ga nA gu ma le sarbA ba ra hUM phaT swA hA/rig pa mchog gis sngag so//oM a ba ga ban sus d+hi bad+mA da kyai haM ma ku ru bad+ma ru ki si dza dzaHspyan 'dren pa'i sngams/
12a6(pa): oM pad+ma sa ma mo ga ga su re ta ra ga sa ma ye s+t+waM ka gdan gyis sngag so//oM a mo g+ha si na ma/sarba tA thA ga ta pU me g+ha hUM ma traM swA hA ka/mchod pa'i sngag so//oM pad+ma bi lo su nam/su tre b+h+yo d+hi swA hA kha saM hi hi d+ha dgyes par bya ba'i sngags/
12a7(pa): de nas gsang ba'i bdag po mchog/rdo rje gsang ba smad myang ba'i//sku gsung thugs dang gsang sngags che byin gyis brlabs pas don bshad mdzad rtsabade bar gshegs pa'i yis mchog/de nyid dkyil 'khor rnam 'bral mdzad/
12b1(pa): dri ma med pa'i byang chub sems//de ni dkyil 'khor snying po mchog/rdo rje gsang ba'i gnas chen po//thabs dang shes rab rol pa'i tshug/rang bzhin byang chub sems nyid wa//rang bzhin lhun gru rdzogs chen po//'gro ba'i snang bas

12b6(pa): sprul pa'i le'u ste gsum pa'o///de nas 'dus pa'i 'khor tshogs rnams//dga' ba chen po'i ngo mtshar gyis//rgyud gsum po dag tshim gyur nas//dgyes pa'i dbyangs kyis gsol ba btag/e ma'o/

13a1(pa): @'byung ba ya mtshan cheka/khro mo 'byung ba yA mtshan che//byin brlabs cho 'khrul ya mtshan che//zla ba rlan mdzad ya mtshan che//'gog pa'i slong mdzad ya mtshan che//bdag cag 'khor tshogs 'dus pa/

13a3(pa): rab tu byang ba'i phyir//rdo rje brgyan gyi ting 'dzin gyi//rang gi dkyil 'khor gsal bar mdzad//de'i tshe byang chub sems chen po//'od zer kun tu khyab pa des//dge' ba chen po'i mchod pa yis//gnas kyi mtshan
13a4(pa): ma gsal bar mdzad//'du shes dang ni 'gog gnas sogs//rang gi rtog pa'i mtha' zhen rnams//dri mo med pa'i byang chub sems//rin chen chu des dbang sgyur ba//rang gi ye shes dbang thobA ste//'gro ba'i don byed nus

13a6(pa): rig par bya//oM gu h+yaM sa ma ya s+t+wa~M//A la la ho//rdo rje rol pas gsungs pa yis//gsung bu dam tshig chen po rnams//rigs kyi bdag pos dgongs pa mdzad//rdo rje gsang ba'i las la gnas//b+hiskal bzang dam tshigs
13a7(pa): chen bu s+ho//de laso 'das na brlag par 'gyur/ci ka'i dge pa dor chen pos//'dus pa'i 'khor tshogs pasas 'ang byas//dam tshig zo nas bral 'khod do//de nas gsang pa'i bdag po nyid/dbang thob las mdza che pos
13b1(pa): bcom ldan 'das la btsug gi phyag/rnal 'byor lug su byas nas ni//phyi nang gsang ba'i mchod pas mchod/mnyes 'gyur gsol ba rab tu gdab//'di ltar rmad byung ngo mtshar khyod//sangs rgyas rnams kyi dbang phyug na//

13b3(pa): bar mtshon ste go bar mdzod//a la la ho snang ba mdzod//ston pa dbang phyug dam pa des//gsol ba btab pa'i don dgongs mdzod//brjod bsam 'das pa'i ting 'dzin la/snyoms par bzhugs nas mi gsung bzhugs
13b4(pa): de nas 'dus pa'i 'khor tshogs kyis//stobs mchog seng+ge chen po la//legasu btude phyag 'tshal nas//rmad byung mchod pa la sogs byas//gsol btab stobs mchog seng+ge khyod//bdag cag zhu ba'i gnas skabs phye//stobs

14a1(pa): thugs rdo rje dngos//sna tshogs gsal zhing snang bar mdzad//de nyid brjod pa'i gsang sngags canaka ba sha las 'das pa'i don de nyid//dgongs don rgyal bas gsung mi mdzad//de ltar ston pa rgyal mchog gis//
14a2(pa): rnar mdzod pa mi spang bas//yang dag don gyis snying po nyid//rgya cher bka' ni bstsal pa yin//'dus pa'i 'khor tshogs rnams nyid cig/byang chub dam pa'i snying po ni//smra bsam las 'das brjod med las//tshigs du brjod pa legs pa yin/
14a3(pa): gnyis mtha'i rtogs pa las gzhan dag/tshig gi snang bas the tshom skye//grol ba nyid kyis mching 'gyur zhes//'das dang ma byon thams cad dang //da ltar byung ba'i dgongs pa'i don//ma gsung mi gsung gsung mi 'gyur//drin sems dpa'i snying po/

14a6(pa): zhu bar spobs//bcom ldan dbang mchog dam pa la//zhus shig spobs mchog seng+ge khyod//des kyang 'khor gyis gsol ba bzhin//rgyal ba rdo 'dzin pa la//phyag 'tshal rmad byung mchod mdzad nas//rdo rje tshigs du 'di skad gsol//kye ma'o//
14a7(pa): ston pa thu ba chen po//phyogs bcu'i rgyal ba kun 'dus pa'i//byang chub sems kyi mtshan nyid de//tshig du brjod pa ma mdzad na//ma 'ongs sgra la brten pa dag/don la reg par mi 'gyur phyir//mtshon bya mtshon par byas nas ni//snying po byang chu

113a3(pa): rin po che'i bdag nyid kun nas kun tu sna tshogs dang /gsung sna tshogs dang /thugs sna tshogs dang /yon tan sna tshogs dang /'phrin las sna tshogs kyis gzhan las 'phags shing /cho 'phrul gyi bya ba thabs mkhas pa'i sgo thams cad rnam pa sna tsho gsugasal
113a4(pa): bar bston pa de nyid/'og min gyi gnas lha'i rgyal po'i khang bzangs dam pa na bzhugs te/'od zer gyi snang ba mngon par 'du mdzad pa sna tshogs 'phrul zhing /dkyil 'khor thams cad rgya phyogs kun tu rgya cher bkod pa mdzad//de nyid kyi tshe na phyogs bcu'i de bzhin gshen
113a5(pa): pa dgra bcom pa yang dag rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams dang /byang chub sems dpa' mtha' yas pa kun nas 'dus pa 'di lta ste/snying po rdo rje zhes bya ba dang /rigs rnam par snang ba zhes bya ba dang /rin po che'i rgyal mtshan zhes bya ba dang /chos thams cad rnam par dag
113a6(pa): pa'i 'khor lo zhes bya ba dang /mdzad pa rgya chen po zhes bya ba dang /pad+mo yod pa zhes bya ba dang /sangs rgyas brtsegs pa zhes bya ba dang /sa rnams gnon pa zhes bya ba dang /'bras bu bskyed pa zhes bya ba dang /las rgyud sel ba zhes bya ba dang /blo gros chen po zhes bya ba dang /
113a7(pa): /thams cad bsrung ba zhes bya ba dang /las kun byed pa zhes bya ba dang /glang po snga can zhes bya ba dang /ston pa gtso bo dam par rnams kyis/snang ba sna tshogs dag rnam par 'phros te/phyogs bcu'i zhing khams thams cad nye bar bskul zhing /de na bzhugs pa'i de bzhin
113b1(pa): gshegs pa thams cad kyi thugs rje chen po nye bar g.yo bar mdzod do//yang dag don ni gyim zhing //rol pa'i byin brlabs kun tu snang /thams cad mi gnyis las kun pos/rigs kyi dkyil 'khor rnam par bkod/de tshe rgyal ba de dag gi dgongs pa'i don kun mnyam/
113b2(pa): par bzhag nas//rang gi ye shes ji ltar bar/rigs kyi dkyil 'khor nye bar bsdud/de ltar 'jig rten gyi khams mtha' yas pa kun nas//sangs rgyas kyi dkyil 'khor ma lus pa thams cad rang rang gi byang chub sems dpa' tshogs dang bcas pas//'og
113b3(pa): min gyi gnasu mngon par lhag so//de dag kyang rang gi dkyil 'khor ji lta ba bzhin tu bzhugs shing //nyid kyi longs spyod thams cad rnam par bkod pa dang /rang gi gdan la thabs dang shes rab rol pa'i tshul gyis thams cad longs spyod par mdza //bcom
113b4(pa): ldan 'das mchog gi dkyil 'khor na/shin tu rnal 'byor dbang phyug tshogs/rgyal ba rgya mtsho 'khor dang bcas/skal ldan rnams dang thabs gcig bzhugs/thams cad don kun snang byed pas/phyag rgya'i dkyil 'khor sna tshogs pa'i//'di
113b5(pa): ldan ston pa kun snang bas//khyad par chos rnams kun gyi gzhi/rang gi ye shes gnas longs spyod /thabs mchog gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor nyid/bde chen dgyes la gsang ba'i sku/kun tu bzang po kun gyi mchog/sangs rgyas kun gyi gtso bo yis/
113b6(pa): 'khor gnas longs spyod pa'o//gnas mchog yum gyi dkyil 'khor na/gnyis med lhun gyis gnas pa'i tshe//de tshe nyan thos dge 'dun dang //rang rgyal theg chen mdo sde dang /byang chub sems dpa'i dge 'dun tshogs/nyan thos dge 'dun chen po'i//dgra btso/
113b7(pa): ming yang med nang du/kun bzangs thugs kyi dkyil 'khor nas/dgongs pa'i don kun rnam par gsal/thabs dang shes rab rol pa'i mthus//ye shes cho 'phrul sna tshogs dang /'od zer snang ba kun khyab byed/phyag rgya'i tshogs kun gar bsgyur cig/'dzum
114a1(pa): mdangs gsang sngags tshig bral ba'i/mi gnyis ye shes kun gsal pa'i/nyid kyi dkyil 'khor thams cad kun/ngo mtsha chos nyid don sa sbyor/dkyil 'khor kun mchog blan med/rigs can mdzad pa ma 'bams pas/sems dang /
114a2(pa): sems gnas rnam par dag/zhing khams bsam gyis mi khyab pa ri/gnas rnams de nyid gnas kun bzhin/de dag ya mtshan chos kun dang /the tsom rgyun gyis rnam por 'jug/gcig la gcig gis brjod par byed pa//'di ltar sangs rgyas kun snang zhing //rang gi
114a3(pa): rtog pa ji ltar bar//don mthun gsang ba 'gro bar byed//gnas kun rigs kyi dkyil 'khor gyis/kun bzang dkyil 'khor rnam par 'phrul//mi mthun sku dang gsung thugs rnams//bya ba'i yon tan rgya cher snang //de dag shes pa la 'das pa//ngo mtshan
114a4(pa): yuyul 'di ni ltar bu/de nas 'khor gyi gtso bo mchog/gsung bdag rdo rje chos nyid kyis/rang gi rigs kyi dkyil 'khor dang /rnam kun stong pa'i gan su 'khod/theg pa gsang ba'i rgyud ltar ba/phyag na rdo rje la gzigs te/rang 'byung bstan pa'i
114a5(pa): sa rnams kun/thabs kyi rgyung pa greng slong mdzad/'didas padas rgyas kun gyi gnas/dacing mdu ma ma dus ci sa nyid/byi dun gyi don//sbyin pa ma thu gyis phyogs pa/
114a6(pa): ba'i bdag pos zhu bar mdzod//thams cad sku dang gsung dang thugs//yon tan 'phrin las sna tshogs kun/mtha' rnams 'gags pa'i don ston pas/thams cad the tshom yid gnyis gyur//de nas gsang ba'i bdag po yis/rang gi rigs tshogs
114a7(pa): dkyil 'khor rnams/rdo rje chos tshogs lhan gcig tu//blo gros gsang ba'i gnas kun 'jug/de nyid phyag rgya ji lta bar/mchod pa bkod tshogs rab 'byams kyis//phyogs bcu'i rgyal ba sras bcas gtso/rang gi byin brlabs yul bcas pa//
114b1(pa): bu tshogs rnam par mchod byas shing /phyag 'tshab 'dud cing gsol pa 'jebs/mgon po dus sum sgrib med pa'i/ye shes dkyil 'khor 'byung ba'i gnas/'di ltar dngos grub rgya chen tshogs/bstan pa'i sgo rnams gang las byung /sangs
114b2(pa): rgyas kun gyi gsang ba'i don//chos kun ma 'dres 'od gsal ba/sgyu ma lta bur 'gag pa med/de dag dkyil 'khor ji lta bur/phyogs rnams dus kun thams cad kyi//sangs rgyas dkyil 'khor zhing dag la/sku gsung thugs kyi phyag rgya rnas
114b3(pa): byin brlabs bkod pa'i yi ge bzhin/rnam 'phrul rnam par spyod pa'i tshogs//de dag chos rnams bstan pa'i dus//shin tu rgya che 'dir gnas pas/ngo mtshar gsang ba'i la zhugs mdzod//de nas sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//rang bzhin kun
114b4(pa): bzang po nyid/nges par bstan pa'i byin ba rlabs kyis//dgyes pa'i dga' mdangs rnam par ston/thams cad rigs kyi dkyil 'khor du//byin brlabs byang chub sems sbyor te/rang bzhin bdud rtsi chen po'i don//nges pa'i klung nas grol
114b5(pa): mdzad//srid gsum ye nas bdud rtsi lnga//ma bcos gdod nas rdzogs sangs rgyas/kun tu bzang po thugs dkyil nas/byang chub sems tshogs dkyil 'khor 'char/dus gsum srid gsum sku gsung thugs//ye nas gnyis med bdud
114b6(pa): ci'i phyir/dgongs pa rgya mtsho mthar phyin pa/des don zab mo tshul gyi 'jug/de nas bde gshegs thams cad kyi/rang snang dkyil 'khor la dgongs ste/gnyis med bdud rtsi chen po 'dir//rang bzhin mi gnyis bstan pas grol//
114b7(pa): khrokhor gyi gtso bo'i mchog/rnal 'byor dbang phyug chen po rnams//blan med pa'i byang chub sems/ye shes mnyam nyid nyams snyed te/rang gi rig pa 'dzin pa'i gnas/rdo rjegs 'i gral rnams 'khod/mtha' las stobs rnams can/
115a1(pa): med de/rgyal bkun gyis mchod byas te/rang bzhin dud kyi chen po gnas/dus gsum thun tu ston par dgos/rin ma tshogs bya chub sems dpa' nams/brtan pa'i don gyis byos skyes ehA'o klu na/
115a2(pa): tshogs kun/rang gi rtog pa mtha' yas snang //kun gyi bsam pa shes rung cing //thams cad skad rnams go 'gyur zhing /theg mchog snying po byin brlabs 'di//phyogs rnams kun nas spyod par dka'/rdzog dkyil 'khor ji lta bar//dun
115a3(pa): tum 'gags rnams sgo mtha' yas/rgyal ba kun gyi lam nyid ni/theg chen bdud rtsi gnas rgyas pas/phyag rgya gsang sngags zab mo'i tshogs/de dag mtshan nyid dza lta bu//de tshe snang ba thams cad kyi//dkyil 'khor ma 'gags rnams
115a4(pa): sprul zhing //rang bzhin gnyis med bdud rtsi'i mchog/bsgrub pa'i thabs 'di nges par gsungs//bstan pa'i phyogs rnams kun spyod/bka'i dam pa mchog gi lung //chos rnams ye nas bdud rtsi'i mchog/nges par bstan pa'i sgo 'di spyong
115a5(pa): sgrub pang bsgrub par bya ba dang /'bras bu thob par bya ba ji phyir/gsang sngags gsang ba'i man ngag ni/bdud rtsi dam pa mchog gi lung //snying pa bsdus pa'i nges don 'dis/thams cad byang chub 'byung ba'i gnas/lta ba dngan
115a6(pa): sbyong ba dang /dam chog mchog dang 'phrin las dang /nges par bsgrub dang bsgrub bya'i phyir//zab mor bsdus pa'i lung ring gsung //bsam gyi mi khyab bstan pa dang /brgyad khri bzhi stong pos ma yas kyang //theg pa'i sgo ni gsung du
115a7(pa): dasungs/gsang nyid zag par 'dus dang bani//ldes por dgrongs dang don po yang /bkri ba doni rasangs rgyas yin/na chung bstan pa zugs bto to//theg ma chos skur khyab pa'i phyir/nges pa'i lung yang spangs rgyas //
115b1(pa): sangs rgyas mnyam dang byings gro gA/yenas gnyis me spyan grub pas//gsapa chen por rnams sbyo cing //rig sa byang chub sems kyi gnas/'di dag 'ang gi brgyad bzhin 'jug/gsang thugs gsang ba'i sangs rgyas /byang
115b2(pa): sems 'di kun tu dag/rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas bde chen nyid/mA lus mtha' dag nyid kyi rgya//ye nas bye byad bya mi dogs//rgyu dang 'bras bu 'khrul pa'i sgra//mi rtog sbyar ba'i yul med na/de lta'i yul dang rtog 'dzin gyis/'khor ba'i
115b3(pa): rgya mtshor gyur pa yin//nges 'byung chos sku chen po la//ba gar ji bzhin bzhag pas na/byang chub nyid lhung gnas med//bu cig ma rtsol bde bar mnos/rgyud dang lung dang man ngag kyang /dmangs dang rje dang rgyal po'i tshul//khyad pa
115b4(pa): rnal 'phags zag ring yang //gcig la gcig rten gcig gi dang /dbyer mang tshul gcig rang bzhin du/gcig ste rnam par yongs kyis med/gnyis mang pa daphyogs sgrol na/bdud rtsi zhes ni de la bya/de bas sgra gsung thugs kyi ba
115b5(pa): khams gsum rnam par spyod pa'i gzhi/sku dang brgyan dang 'khor lo yang //lung chen thas mo nang nags pa//wa bzhi'i nang du la khor ba las/ye shes sangs rgyas gzhan na med/dbyings su gyur pas mnyam po dadra//de ni sangs rgyas med chemanyid/
115b6(pa): bdud rtsi bdi chen mchog hU~M//dus gsum 'gyur med dbyings n+d+he rgya/dbyings 'gyur drug mnyam lhan grub pas//rnal 'byor chen po'i ngadu tshul bzang /lta ba 'di la gang gnas bir//rnal 'byor mchog gyur skal bzang po//ni spyod kyi sgrom rdzi
115b7(pa): kyang dang bdud rtsi nyid de sman gyi mchog/bder gshegs kun gyi thugs dam nyid//byin brlabs ngo mtshar rgya cher 'byung //rgyal ba rgya mtsho rnams kyi gsas//sangs rgyas bde chen nyid las 'byung //thams cad ma lus bdud rtsi 'odi//spos me
116a1(pa): thig ler gnas pa las//mtha' yas sems can don gyi phyir//rang la mchod cing rang gis bza'//de dag dngos grub rgya mtsho'i gzhi/bsgrub pa pos ni nan tan byos//thams cad rang 'byung bdud rtsir gnas//chos rnams kun gyi
116a2(pa): de bzhin nyid//ston pa'i byin brlabs la snang ba//rnal 'byor thabs mkhas rnams kyi yul//rang bzhin ngo bo cig nyid las/byin brlabs mthu rtsal rnams spros pas/thams cad dag cing grol ba'i mchog/bdud rtsi chen po'i rang bzhin te//thabs
116a3(pa): mkhas rnam par spyod pa'i sgo/rang bzhin ngo bos ston byed pa'o//byang chub sems kyi dkyil 'khor du//phyi nang snod bcud rnam dag tshogs/rang grol 'jug pa'i spyod pa yi//dkyil 'khor chen po'i gnas mthong 'gyur//de dag rab tu dbang bskur

116a7(pa): gsang chen nyid kyi dbyings//rnam grol ye shes dkyil 'khor nyid//byang chub sems nyid rnam spyod pas/rin chen bdud rtsi rgyas par snang //phyogs rnams mtha' dag grol ba'i//rigs kun ngo mtshar skye ba'i gnas//rgyal ba rgya mtsho rnams kyi gnas
116b1(pa): rang byung ye shes gnas phyag 'tshal//thams cad min la thams cad snang //de dag rang gi dkyil 'khor nyid//phyogs dus kun tu ma 'gags pa'i/yon tan kun 'byung gnas phyag 'tshal//ngo mtshar zab cing rgya chen chos/rigs tshogs theg pa grang /
116b2(pa): med kyis//de dag tshul rnams zhi mdzad pa/dkyil 'khor kun 'byung ston pa'i mchog/khyod nyid byang chub sems kyi zhing /gsang chen dkyil 'khor rdzogs pa nyid//gang zhig mthong bas rnam grol zhing //phyag rgya'i dkyil 'khor kun spyod mdzad//byin
116b3(pa): brlabs theg pa'i gnas chen 'di//rgyal ba kun gyi bstan pa'i don//phyag rgya'i rol pa rgya chen por//thams cad 'dud cing ston pa'i gnas/zhes kun gyis rgya cher ston to//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis rang gi dkyil 'khor mngon par

116b6(pa): ba kun kyang tshul sna tshogs kyi sgo nas spyad par snang ngo ///gdong nas ma bcos sangs rgyas kun//rang gi gnas na longs spyod mdzad//de la grangs med rgya chen tshogs//zhing khams bsam gyis mi khyab grub//skye 'gro kun
116b7(pa): gyi gnas spyod de/tha dad gnyis kyi chos snang ba'i//'di ltar kha cig brtags pa'i rgyud//nyon mongs rgyu las de bskyed cing //nga rgyal chen pos gnas pa la/nga rgyal tshogs bcas rags par spyod//de dag gnyis dang gsum bcas pa'i///
117a1(pa): dregs pa'i mtha' kun mngon du gnyer//de'i rgyu dang rkyen dag dang //'bras bu nyid kyis gnas rgyas 'gyur//de las nyon mongs tshogs bskyar cing //lha min grong khyer la sogs pa'i//nga rgyal dregs pa'i mtha' gnas pa//rang

117a6(pa): po kha gcig tu//zhe sdang chos kun rnam spyod de//de nyid kun nas zhen pa'i mthus//nyon mongs chos kun bskyed par byed//de dag rgyu bcas 'bras bu nyid//dmyal ba'i 'jig rten la sogs snang //rgyu rkyen goms shugs mthar phyin pas//rtag
117a7(pa): tu rang gi las bzhin spyod//sdug bsngal sdug bsngal sna tshogs kyis/bar 'tshams med par kun tu rmongaso//de nyid rkyen gyis gzhan dag kyang //de'i bstan pa 'dzud cing //kun nas nyes pa'i lta ba yis/mtha' rnams kun tu rnam par myos//zhing

117b2(pa): 'dzin pas bdag bsgrub byed//gzhan rtogs gnas kun phrag dog nyid//nyon mongs sdug bsngal rgyu bcas pa'i//yan lag gzhan tshogs rnam bskyed pas//rtogs pa'i chos rnams rab tu rgyas/de las rtsa ba yan lag go//rgyu bcas kyi ming sogs
117b3(pa): gnas//rang gi mthu yis kun tu snang //dus kun mngon par zhen pa'i shugs//gzhan dag bstan pa la gnas byed//mngon par 'pho ba'i las kun gyis/lha la sogs pa'i 'gro ba kun//rang gi las ngang las kyis 'khor//de las sdug
117b4(pa): bsngal kun snang bas//phyogs rnams kun tu rgyas par 'gyur//de ltar nyon mongs tshogs 'phel zhing //rtsa ba mngon bskyed 'byin byed pas//yan lag cha shas rnam rgyal te//'gro gzhan gnas kun rgyas par 'grub//kha cig de dag kun 'dzin
